53 // Make compile time checks if blas::gemv can handle the given input vector. If it can, simply forward the vector.
54 // Otherwise, return a copy with the given value type T and container policy CP.
55 template <typename T, typename CP, Vector X>
56 97 decltype(auto) get_gemv_vector(X &&x) {
57 if constexpr (std::is_same_v<get_value_t<X>, T> and MemoryVector<X>) {
58 89 return std::forward<X>(x);
59 } else {
60 8 return vector<T, CP>{x};
61 }
62 }

65 // Otherwise, return a copy with the given value type T and container policy CP.

92 /**
93 * @brief Compute the matrix-vector product of an nda::Matrix and an nda::Vector object.
94 *
95 * @details This function computes the matrix-vector product
96 * \f[
97 * \mathbf{y} = \mathbf{A} \mathbf{x} \; ,
98 * \f]
99 * where \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ is an \f$ m \times n \f$ matrix and \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ and \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ are vectors
100 * of size \f$ n \f$ and \f$ m \f$, respectively.
101 *
102 * The behaviour of this function is similar to nda::blas::gemv, except that it allows
103 * - lazy expressions as input,
104 * - the value types of the input matrix and vector to be different from each other and
105 * - the value types of the input matrix and vector to be different from nda::is_blas_lapack_v.
106 *
107 * We try to call nda::blas::gemv whenever possible, i.e. when the value type of the result is compatible with
108 * nda::is_blas_lapack_v, even if this requires to make copies of the input arrays/views. Otherwise, we perform a very
109 * naive and inefficient matrix-vector multiplication manually.
110 *
111 * Therefore, if performance is important, users should make sure to pass input arrays/views which are compatible with
112 * nda::blas::gemv.
113 *
114 * The value type of the resulting nda::vector is deduced from the multiplication of the value types of the input
115 * arguments and its address space is set to their nda::mem::common_addr_space.
116 *
117 * This function might make copies of the input arrays/views. When working on the device memory space, this may lead
118 * to runtime errors if the copying fails.
119 *
120 * @note \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ and \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ are required to satisfy nda::mem::have_compatible_addr_space.
121 *
122 * @tparam A nda::Matrix type.
123 * @tparam X nda::Vector type.
124 * @param a Input matrix \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ of size \f$ m \times n \f$.
125 * @param x Input vector \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ of size \f$ n \f$.
126 * @return Resulting nda::vector \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ of size \f$ m \f$.
127 */
128 template <Matrix A, Vector X>
129 requires(mem::have_compatible_addr_space<A, X>)
130 109 auto matvecmul(A const &a, X const &x) {
131 // get the return type
132 using value_t = decltype(a(0, 0) * x(0));
133 using cont_pol = heap<mem::common_addr_space<A, X>>;
134 using return_t = vector<value_t, cont_pol>;
135
136 // result vector (MSAN complains if it is not initialized)
137
8/16
